LLM supporting:  the provided context does not directly address the specific dna strand targeted by transcription - coupled repair ( tcr ). however, based on the general understanding of tcr, it primarily targets the non - template ( non - sense ) strand of dna. this is because tcr is a mechanism that specifically recognizes and corrects errors in the transcribed strand, which is the strand that is being read by rna polymerase during transcription. the non - template strand, also known as the coding strand, is the one that is complementary to the template strand and contains the sequence that is transcribed into mrna. therefore, when a mutation or lesion occurs in the transcribed strand, tcr can identify and repair it, ensuring the accuracy of the transcribed mrna. this targeted repair is crucial for maintaining the integrity of gene expression and preventing the propagation of errors in the genetic code.
